{"forms":[{"form":"neuters","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"n.","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]},{"form":"n","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]},{"form":"ntr","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An organism, either vegetable or animal, which at its maturity has no generative organs, or but imperfectly developed ones, as a plant without stamens or pistils, as the garden Hydrangea; especially, one of the imperfectly developed females of certain social insects, as of the ant and the common honeybee, which perform the labors of the community, and are called workers."],"topics":["biology","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["One who has been neutered; eunuch."],"tags":["offensive","sometimes","uncommon"]},{"glosses":["The act of neutering (typically an animal)"]},{"glosses":["A person who takes no part in a contest; someone remaining neutral."]},{"glosses":["The neuter gender."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A noun of the neuter gender; any one of those words which have the terminations usually found in neuter words."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An intransitive verb or state-of-being verb."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈnjuːtə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈn(j)utɚ/","tags":["US"]}],"upstream_line":16825,"upstream_offset":113696261,"upstream_record_sha256":"fdddf83d2f9bf19a09e5627c96cd9be314a24f6cf93cbfb1db1f9a632d7ac390","word":"neuter"}
